Accepts --full, --path &lt;dir&gt;, --runtime, --parent-branch &lt;branch&gt;. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-7-accessibility.md))

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/analyze.md) · ★1)

Retrieves the most relevant notes (lessons, topics, conversations) lexically, then synthesizes an answer with cited [[wikilink]] sources via `claude -p`. Usage — `/sb:ask "what did I learn about X?"`. Supports `tag:#area/sub` filters in the query. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ask.md) · ★1)

The safe complement to /sb:search. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ask-highlights.md) · ★1)
- **sb:backfill**: One-shot import of historical Claude Code conversations from `~/.claude/projects/*.jsonl` into the vault. NOT run automatically. - **sb:challenge**: Argue against a note using your own prior notes — a skeptical devil's-advocate pass that appends a ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/challenge.md) · ★1)
- **sb:clean**: Vault cleanup. Dry-run by default; pass `--apply` to hard-delete. Removes empty conversations, garbage tags, stale session-map entries. - **sb:consolidate**: Bound vault growth — dedupe lessons, flag stale conversations + orphans, promote durable lessons into harness memory. Dry-run by default; --apply to merge/archive (never hard-deletes). ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/consolidate.md) · ★1)
- **sb:daily**: Show today's second-brain activity — conversations updated, lessons created, tasks added/done, topics touched. No LLM, instant. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/daily.md) · ★1)
- **sb:dashboard**: Regenerate the Life-Dashboard homepage (00_Dashboard/Home.md + Today.md) — embedded Bases (review queue, tasks, decisions, people, meetings), Dataview recent lessons/zettels, habit streaks, and memory/remember highlights. Points the homepage plugin at Home.md. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/dashboard.md) · ★1)
- **sb:decision**: Capture an architecture decision (ADR) as an AI-first vault note — numbered Context/Decision/Consequences/Usage under 02_Projects/&lt;slug&gt;/decisions/ plus a global 11_Decisions copy. Use for "why did we choose", ADRs, and council verdicts. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/decision.md) · ★1)

Writes a verified:false distillation note to 08_Insights/ with sources preserved verbatim. Use to compress a long lesson/topic into traceable facts. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/distill.md) · ★1)

Dry-run by default; --apply writes. Use to compound scattered notes into higher-order insights. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/emerge.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/eval.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/export.md) · ★1)
- **sb:find**: Fuzzy filename/title lookup across the vault. Usage — `/sb:find &lt;fragment&gt;`. Fast, no LLM. For full-text content search use /sb:search; for Q&A use /sb:ask. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/find.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/graduate.md) · ★1)

- **sb:habit**: Habit tracker — log a habit as done today (with streak) or list all habits and their current streaks. Use for "did X today", "track habit Y", "show my streaks". ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/habit.md) · ★1)

- **sb:idea**: Capture an idea into 16_Ideas/ (type: idea, status: captured). List bullet points in --body and they become the graduation checklist. Use for "I have an idea", "capture this idea", "note this for later" — a lightweight seed you can later /sb:graduate into a real project. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/idea.md) · ★1)
- **sb:ingest**: Write a supplied set of notes into the vault (generic, skill-agnostic). Reads a JSON payload from stdin or --payload &lt;file&gt; and writes each note as a typed project note, optionally refreshing an open-issues section. Used by the sutra orchestrator to land parsed repo artifacts in the vault; usable directly for any structured note payload. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ingest.md) · ★1)
- **sb:init**: (Re)generate the vault's machine-facing entry docs — _CLAUDE.md (folder map, note types, AI-first/verified rules, command list) and index.md (per-folder counts, entry points, Base links). Idempotent and sentinel-bounded; hand-added content outside the sentinels survives. Use after adding folders/commands or to refresh the guide. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/init.md) · ★1)
- **sb:insights**: Cross-project pattern detection — surfaces tags that appear in ≥ 2 lessons across ≥ 2 projects ("you've hit auth issues 4 times across 3 projects"). Writes `connections/recurring-themes.md`. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/insights.md) · ★1)
- **sb:kanban**: Print or open the project's kanban board. Usage — `/sb:kanban [--project &lt;slug&gt;] [--open]`. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/kanban.md) · ★1)

- **sb:meeting**: Create a meeting note with auto-linked attendees — Agenda/Notes/Decisions/Action Items, attendees linked to People notes (stubs created if missing) with interaction-log entries. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/person.md) · ★1)
- **sb:project**: List all second-brain projects with stats, or print a specific project's INDEX.md. Usage: /sb:project [&lt;slug&gt;] ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/project.md) · ★1)
- **sb:recall**: Surface relevant past lessons/topics/connections for the current conversation's context (last 3000 chars + tags). No LLM, instant. Useful when starting to dig into a problem to see "what do I already know?". ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/recall.md) · ★1)
- **sb:related**: Show the semantically nearest notes to a given note using smart-connections' precomputed vectors (key-free, note-to-note cosine). ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/related.md) · ★1)
- **sb:review**: Show stale content (topics not touched in N days, un-analyzed conversations, long-open tasks). Encourages weeding the garden. Usage — `/sb:review [--days=30]`.
